<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If Tom is half his mum's age and together their ages add up to $72$, how old is Tom?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If Tom is half his mum's age and together their ages add up to $72$, how old is Tom?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Tom's age $= a$

Tom's mum's age $=2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 2a      &= 72 \\\\
3a          &= 72 \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {72} {3} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {24 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a           &= \dfrac {24 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
a           &= 24 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

Tom's age $= 24 \ \text{years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$24 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Arthur's twin sisters are $4 \ \text {years}$  older than him. Altogether their ages add up to $77$. How old is Arthur?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Arthur's twin sisters are $4 \ \text {years}$  older than him. Altogether their ages add up to $77$. How old is Arthur?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Arthur's age $= a$

His twin sisters' ages $= a + 4$ and $a + 4$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + (a + 4) + (a + 4)   &= 77 \\\\
3a + 8                  &= 77 \\\\
3a                      &= 77 - 8 \\\\
3a                      &= 69 \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac{69}{3} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac{23 \times 3}{3} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac{23 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
a                       &= 23 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$23 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Claire is two years older than her brother and five years younger than her sister. 
The total of their ages is $87$ years.

How old is Claire?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Claire is two years older than her brother and five years younger than her sister. 
The total of their ages is $87$ years.
<emptyline>
How old is Claire?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Claire's age $= a$

Her brother's age $= a - 2$

Her sister's age $= a + 5$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a - 2 + a + 5   &= 87 \\\\
3a + 3              &= 87 \\\\
3a                  &= 87 - 3 \\\\
3a                  &= 84 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {84} {3} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {28 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {28 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                   &= 28 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Claire's age $= 28 \ \text {years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>

3a                  &= 87 - 3 \\\\

a                   &= \dfrac {28 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {28 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                   &= 28 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Claire's age $= 28 \ \text {years}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mr Patel and his sons Humza and Kian have the same birthday.

Today, Mr Patel is $34$, Humza is $6$ and Kian is $3$. 

How old will they be when Mr Patel's age is the sum of his sons' ages?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mr Patel and his sons Humza and Kian have the same birthday.
<emptyline>
Today, Mr Patel is $34$, Humza is $6$ and Kian is $3$. 
<emptyline>
How old will they be when Mr Patel's age is the sum of his sons' ages?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of years ahead $= a$

Mr Patel's age $= 34 + a$

Humza's age $= 6 + a$

Kian's age $= 3 + a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
34 + a                  &= 6 + a + 3 + a \\\\
34 + a                  &= 9 + 2a \\\\
2a + 9                  &= 34 + a \\\\
2a - a                  &= 34 - 9 \\\\
a                       &= 25 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Mr Patel's age}   &= 34 + a \\\\
                        &= 34 + 25  \\\\
                        &= 59 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Hamza's age}      &= 6 + a \\\\
                        &= 6 + 25  \\\\
                        &= 31 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Kian's age}       &= 3 + a \\\\
                        &= 3 + 25  \\\\
                        &= 28 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Mr Patel $=$ $59 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Hamza $=$ $31 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Kian $=$ $28 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When Patience asked her Grandfather how old he was, he answered, 

“I am $34$ now and will be $33$ on my birthday.”

She looked confused so he added, “After being $50$ for 
a year I started counting backwards on each birthday.”

How old will he actually be on his birthday?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When Patience asked her Grandfather how old he was, he answered, 
<emptyline>
“I am $34$ now and will be $33$ on my birthday.”
<emptyline>
She looked confused so he added, “After being $50$ for 
a year I started counting backwards on each birthday.”
<emptyline>
How old will he actually be on his birthday?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Grandfather's age}   &= 50 + (50 - 33) \\\\
                            &= 50 + 17 \\\\
                            &= 67 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$67 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                            &= 67 \ \text {years}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Guy is half as old as Gerald, who is three times older than Gill. If the sum of all of their ages is $99$, what are their ages?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Guy is half as old as Gerald, who is three times older than Gill. If the sum of all of their ages is $99$, what are their ages?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Gill's age $= a$

Gerald's age $= 3a$

Guy's age $= \dfrac {3a} {2}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3a + \dfrac {3a} {2} + a                                        &= 99 \\\\
4a + \dfrac {3a} {2}                                            &= 99 \\\\
\dfrac {4a \times 2} {2} + \dfrac {3a} {2}                      &= 99 \\\\
\dfrac {8a} {2} + \dfrac {3a} {2}                               &= 99 \\\\
\dfrac {11a} {2}                                                &= 99 \\\\
a                                                               &= \dfrac {99 \times 2} {11} \\\\
a                                                               &= \dfrac {11 \times 9 \times 2} {11} \\\\
a                                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel {11} \times 9 \times 2} {\cancel {11}} \\\\
a                                                               &= 18 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Gill's age $= 18 \ \text {years}$

Let's find Gerald's and Guy's ages.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Gerald's age}    &= 3a \\\\
                        &= 3 \times 18 \\\\
                        &= 54 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Guy's age}       &= \dfrac {3a} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 18} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 9} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {2} \times 9} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= 27 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Gill $=$ $18 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Gerald $=$ $54 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Guy $=$ $27 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Anna is $3 \ \text{years}$ younger than Brian. Brian is $7 \ \text{years}$ older than Callum.

How much older is Anna than Callum?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Anna is $3 \ \text{years}$ younger than Brian. Brian is $7 \ \text{years}$ older than Callum.
<emptyline>
How much older is Anna than Callum?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Brian's age $= a$

Anna's age $= a - 3$

Callum's age $= a - 7$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference between Anna and Callum} &= (a - 3) - (a - 7) \\\\
                                          &= a - 3 -a + 7 \\\\
                                          &= 4 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Derek is $15 \ \text{years}$ old. His Dad is three times his age.
Derek's brother, Dave, is six year younger than him.

How many times older is Dad than Dave?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Derek is $15 \ \text{years}$ old. His Dad is three times his age.
Derek's brother, Dave, is six year younger than him.
<emptyline>
How many times older is Dad than Dave?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Derek's age $= 15 \ \text{years}$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Dad versus Dave}  &= \dfrac {\text {Derek's dad's age}} {\text {Dave's age}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {15 \times 3} {15 - 6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {15 \times 3} {9} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 3} {3 \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {3}} \\\\
                        &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

Derek's dad is $5$ times older than Dave.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Emily is one quarter the age of her Aunt Ingrid. Their ages total $60$. How old is Emily?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Emily is one quarter the age of her Aunt Ingrid. Their ages total $60$. How old is Emily?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Emily's age $= a$

Ingrid's age $= 4a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 4a &= 60 \\\\
5a     &= 60 \\\\
a      &= \dfrac {60} {5} \\\\
a      &= \dfrac {12 \times 5} {5} \\\\
a      &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
a      &= 12 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

Emily's age $= 12 \ \text{years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sanjay is two years younger than Sara, who is five years older than Sasha. Sanjay's age and Sasha's age total to $31$. How old is Sara?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sanjay is two years younger than Sara, who is five years older than Sasha. Sanjay's age and Sasha's age total to $31$. How old is Sara?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Sara's age $= a$

Sanjay's age $= a - 2$

Sasha's age $= a - 5$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a - 2 + a  -5     &= 31 \\\\
2a - 7            &= 31 \\\\
2a                &= 31 + 7 \\\\
2a                &= 38 \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {38} {2} \\\\
a                 &= 19 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

Sara's age $= 19 \ \text{years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$19 \ \text{years}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In four years time, Sam will be twice as old as he was four years ago. Calculate Sam's current age.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In four years time, Sam will be twice as old as he was four years ago. Calculate Sam's current age.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Sam's current age $=a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 4     &= 2(a - 4) \\\\
a + 4     &= 2a - 8 \\\\
2a - a    &= 8 + 4 \\\\
a         &= 12 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

Sam's current age $=12 \ \text{years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let Sam's current age $=a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + 4     &= 2(a - 4) \\\\
a + 4     &= 2a - 8 \\\\
2a - a    &= 8 + 4 \\\\
a         &= 12 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Sam's current age $=12 \ \text{years}$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

My age is a multiple of $7$. Next year it will be a multiple of $5$. I am more than $20$ years old, but less than $80$. How old am I? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">My age is a multiple of $7$. Next year it will be a multiple of $5$. I am more than $20$ years old, but less than $80$. How old am I? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

We can easily infer the answer by using trial and error.

Let's find the number that meets the following conditions:

- The age lies between $20$ and $80$.
- It is a multiple of $7$.
- The age next year is a multiple of $5$.

The table below shows the possible ages:

| Age | Age $+1$ | Multiple of $5$? |
|:---:|:--------:|:----------------:|
| $7 \times 3 = 21$ | $22$ | No |
| $7 \times 4 = 28$ | $29$ | No |
| $7 \times 5 = 35$ | $36$ | No |
| $7 \times 6 = 42$ | $43$ | No |
| $7 \times 7 = 49$ | $50$ | Yes |
| $7 \times 8 = 56$ | $57$ | No |
| $7 \times 9 = 63$ | $64$ | No |
| $7 \times 10 = 70$ | $71$ | No |
| $7 \times 11 = 77$ | $78$ | No |

There is only one number satisfying all the conditions.

Age $= 49 \ \text {years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$49 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Abigail is three time as old as Ryan. In $6$ years time Abigail will be $42$.

How old will Ryan will be in $2$ years?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Abigail is three time as old as Ryan. In $6$ years time Abigail will be $42$.
<emptyline>
How old will Ryan will be in $2$ years?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Ryan's current age $= a$

Abigail's current age $= 3a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3a + 6                      &= 42 \\\\
3a                          &= 42 - 6 \\\\
3a                          &= 36 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {36} {3} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                           &= 12 \ \text{years} 
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Ryan's age after } 2 \text{ years }    &= 12 + 2 \\\\
                                            &= 14 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$14 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

My father was $31$ on my $8^\text{th}$ birthday.
How old will he be  on my $30^\text{th}$ birthday? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">My father was $31$ on my $8^\text{th}$ birthday.
How old will he be  on my $30^\text{th}$ birthday? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Father's age on 30}^\text{th} \text{ birthday}   &= 31 + (30 - 8) \\\\
                                                        &= 31 + 22 \\\\
                                                        &= 53 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$53 \ \text{years}$

</div>

</div>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let David's age $= a$

Mark's age $= a + 3$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 3                &= 89 \\\\
2 a + 3                  &= 89 \\\\
2 a                      &= 89 - 3 \\\\
2 a                      &= 86 \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {86} {2} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {43 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                        &= \dfrac {43 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                        &= 43 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mark's age} &= a + 3 \\\\
                  &= 43 + 3 \\\\
                  &= 46 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

An oak tree was planted
in the year in which
Lord Owlynose was born.
He died in $1940$, aged $80$.
How old was the oak tree
in $2011?$

- $150$ 
- $151$ 
- $152$ 
- $153$ 
- $155$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">An oak tree was planted
in the year in which
Lord Owlynose was born.
He died in $1940$, aged $80$.
How old was the oak tree
in $2011?$
<emptyline>
- $150$ 
- $151$ 
- $152$ 
- $153$ 
- $155$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Tree's age in 2011}   &= \text {Lord's age at death} + (2011 - \text {Year Lord died}) \\\\
                             &= 80 + (2011 - 1940) \\\\
                             &= 80 + 71 \\\\
                             &= 151 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$151 \ \text {years}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A woman's age is $70\ \text {years}$, $70\ \text{months}$, $70\ \text{weeks}$  and $70\ \text{days}$. How old will she be on her next birthday?

- $71$
- $76$
- $78$
- $80$
- $81$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A woman's age is $70\ \text {years}$, $70\ \text{months}$, $70\ \text{weeks}$  and $70\ \text{days}$. How old will she be on her next birthday?
<emptyline>
- $71$
- $76$
- $78$
- $80$
- $81$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Woman's current age}     &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 70 \ \text {weeks} + 70 \ \text {days} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 70 \ \text {weeks} + \dfrac {70} {7} \ \text {weeks} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 70 \ \text {weeks} + 10 \ \text {weeks} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 80 \ \text {weeks} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + \dfrac {80} {4} \ \text {months} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 20 \ \text {months} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 90 \ \text {months} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + \dfrac {90} {12} \ \text {years} \\\\
                                &= 70 + \dfrac {15} {2} \\\\
                                &= 70 + 7 \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                                &= 77 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Age on her next birthday} &= 78 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$78 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Woman's current age}     &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 70 \ \text {weeks} + 70 \ \text {days} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 70 \ \text {weeks} + \dfrac {70} {7} \ \text {weeks} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 70 \ \text {weeks} + 10 \ \text {weeks} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 80 \ \text {weeks} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + \dfrac {80} {4} \ \text {months} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 70 \ \text {months} + 20 \ \text {months} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + 90 \ \text {months} \\\\
                                &= 70 \ \text {years} + \dfrac {90} {12} \ \text {years} \\\\
                                &= 70 + \dfrac {15} {2} \\\\
                                &= 70 + 7 \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                                &= 77 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Age on her next birthday} &= 78 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On $1 ^\text{st}$ January $2006$ my grandmother was $80 \ \text{years}$ old. Her daughter was $40 \ \text{years}$ old on $1 ^\text{st}$ January $1994$. 
How old was my grandmother when her daughter was born?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On $1 ^\text{st}$ January $2006$ my grandmother was $80 \ \text{years}$ old. Her daughter was $40 \ \text{years}$ old on $1 ^\text{st}$ January $1994$. 
How old was my grandmother when her daughter was born?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Grandmother's age in 2006}               &= 80 \\\\
\text {Daughter's age in 2006}                  &= 40 + (2006 - 1994) \\\\
                                                &= 40 + 12 \\\\
                                                &= 52 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Grandmother's age at daughter's birth}    &= 80 - 52 \\\\
                                                &= 28 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

                                                &= 28 \ \text {years}

$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Mrs Jones is $24 \ \text{years}$ older than her daughter. The sum of their ages is $70 \ \text{years}$. How old is Mrs Jones?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Mrs Jones is $24 \ \text{years}$ older than her daughter. The sum of their ages is $70 \ \text{years}$. How old is Mrs Jones?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Mrs Jones age $=a$

Her daughter's age $= a - 24$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a - 24 &= 70 \\\\
2 a        &= 70 + 24 \\\\
2 a        &= 94 \\\\
a          &= \dfrac {94} {2} \\\\
a          &= \dfrac {47 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a          &= \dfrac {47 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a          &= 47 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Mrs Jones age $= 47 \ \text {years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$47 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Four years ago the combined age of $3$ dogs was $24$ years.

What will the combined age be in $2$ year's time?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Four years ago the combined age of $3$ dogs was $24$ years.
<emptyline>
What will the combined age be in $2$ year's time?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Combined age in} \ 2 \ \text {years time}    &= 24 + (4 + 2) \times 3 \\\\
                                                    &= 24 + 6 \times 3 \\\\
                                                    &= 24 + 18 \\\\
                                                    &= 42 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$42 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Richard is $12$ years old. Adam is twice as old as Richard. Will is $\dfrac{2}{3}$  of Adam's age.
        
What is their total age?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Richard is $12$ years old. Adam is twice as old as Richard. Will is $\dfrac{2}{3}$  of Adam's age.
<emptyline>
What is their total age?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Richard's age}    &= 12 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Adam's age}       &= 2 \times \text {Richard's age} \\\\
                         &= 2 \times 12 \\\\
                         &= 24 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Will's age}       &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times \text {Adam's age} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 24 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {2 \times 24} {3} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {2 \times 8 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {2 \times 8 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                         &= 16 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Total age}        &= 12 + 24 + 16 \\\\
                         &= 52 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$52 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

                         &= 2 \times 12 \\\\

                         &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 24 \\\\

                         &= \dfrac {2 \times 8 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\

$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Richard is $18$ years old.

Adam is half as old as Richard.

Will is $\dfrac{2}{3}$ of Adam's age.

What is their total age?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Richard is $18$ years old.
<emptyline>
Adam is half as old as Richard.
<emptyline>
Will is $\dfrac{2}{3}$ of Adam's age.
<emptyline>
What is their total age?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Richard's age}    &= 18 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Adam's age}       &= \dfrac {1} {2} \text{ of Richard's age} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {18} {2} \\\\
                        &= 9 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Will's age}       &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times \text{Adam's age} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 9 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 9} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                        &= 6 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Total age}        &= 18 + 9 + 6 \\\\
                        &= 33 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$33 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\begin{aligned}
\text{Richard's age}    &= 18 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Adam's age}       &= \dfrac {1} {2} \text{ of Richard's age} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {18} {2} \\\\
                        &= 9 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Will's age}       &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times \text{Adam's age} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {3} \times 9 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 9} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times \cancel{3}} {\cancel{3}} \\\\
                        &= 6 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Total age}        &= 18 + 9 + 6 \\\\
                        &= 33 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Eliot is now twice his cousin's age. In $4 \ \text{years'}$ time Eliot will be $16$. How old will his cousin be then?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Eliot is now twice his cousin's age. In $4 \ \text{years'}$ time Eliot will be $16$. How old will his cousin be then?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Eliot's current age $= a$

His cousin's current age $= \dfrac {a} {2}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 4   &= 16 \\\\
a       &= 16 - 4 \\\\
a       &= 12 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cousin's age in } 4   \text { years}     &= \dfrac {a} {2} + 4 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {12} {2} + 4 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6 \times 2} {2} + 4 \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} + 4 \\\\
                                                &= 6 + 4 \\\\
                                                &= 10 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

                                                &= \dfrac {12} {2} + 4 \\\\

                                                &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} + 4 \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Today Jack is twice as old as Jill. In ten years' time Jack will be one and a half times as old as Jill. How old will Jack and Jill be in twenty years' time?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Today Jack is twice as old as Jill. In ten years' time Jack will be one and a half times as old as Jill. How old will Jack and Jill be in twenty years' time?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Jill's current age $= a$

Jack's current age $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2a + 10                                                 &= 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \times (a + 10) \\\\
2a + 10                                                 &= \dfrac {3} {2} \times (a + 10) \\\\
2 \times (2a + 10)                                      &= 3 \times (a + 10) \\\\
4a + 20                                                 &= 3a + 30 \\\\
4a - 3a                                                 &= 30  - 20 \\\\
a                                                       &= 10 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Jack's age in } 20 \text{ years}                  &= 2a + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 2 \times 10 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 40 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Jill's age in } 20 \text{ years}                  &= a + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 10 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 30 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Jack $=$ $40 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Jill $=$ $30 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>

4a - 3a                                                 &= 30  - 20 \\\\
a                                                       &= 10 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Jack's age in } 20 \text{ years}                  &= 2a + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 2 \times 10 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 40 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Jill's age in } 20 \text{ years}                  &= a + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 10 + 20 \\\\
                                                        &= 30 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A man is $30  \ \text{years}$ older than his youngest son. In $17 \ \text{years}$ he will be twice his son's age. How old is the son?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A man is $30  \ \text{years}$ older than his youngest son. In $17 \ \text{years}$ he will be twice his son's age. How old is the son?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the man's age $= a$

His son's  age $= a - 30$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 17              &= 2 \times (a - 30 + 17) \\\\
a + 17              &= 2 \times (a - 13) \\\\
a + 17              &= 2a - 26 \\\\
2a - 26             &= a + 17 \\\\
2a - a              &= 17 + 26 \\\\
a                   &= 43 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Son's age}   &= a - 30 \\\\
                    &= 43 - 30 \\\\
                    &= 13 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$13 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The total of the ages of a father, his daughter and his two twin sons is $96 \ \text{years}$.  The daughter's age is half the father's age and twice the age of each of her two brothers. 

How old is the father? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The total of the ages of a father, his daughter and his two twin sons is $96 \ \text{years}$.  The daughter's age is half the father's age and twice the age of each of her two brothers. 
<emptyline>
How old is the father? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the father's age $= a$

Daughter's age $= \dfrac {a} {2}$

Brothers' ages $= \dfrac{a} {4}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + \dfrac {a} {2} + \dfrac {a} {4} + \dfrac {a} {4}                                            &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {a \times 4} {4} + \dfrac {a \times 2} {2 \times 2} + \dfrac{a}{4} + \dfrac{a}{4}        &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {4a} {4} + \dfrac{2a}{4} + \dfrac{a}{4} + \dfrac{a}{4}                                   &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {4a + 2a + a + a} {4}                                                                    &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {8a} {4}                                                                                 &= 96 \\\\
a                                                                                               &= \dfrac{96 \times 4} {8} \\\\
a                                                                                               &= \dfrac{12 \times 8 \times 4} {8} \\\\
a                                                                                               &= \dfrac{12 \times \cancel {8} \times 4} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
a                                                                                               &= 12 \times 4 \\\\
a                                                                                               &= 48 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Father's age $= 48 \ \text {years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the father's age $= a$
<emptyline>
Daughter's age $= \dfrac {a} {2}$
<emptyline>
Brothers' ages $= \dfrac{a} {4}$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + \dfrac {a} {2} + \dfrac {a} {4} + \dfrac {a} {4}                                            &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {a \times 4} {4} + \dfrac {a \times 2} {2 \times 2} + \dfrac{a}{4} + \dfrac{a}{4}        &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {4a} {4} + \dfrac{2a}{4} + \dfrac{a}{4} + \dfrac{a}{4}                                   &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {4a + 2a + a + a} {4}                                                                    &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {8a} {4}                                                                                 &= 96 \\\\
a                                                                                               &= \dfrac{96 \times 4} {8} \\\\
a                                                                                               &= \dfrac{12 \times 8 \times 4} {8} \\\\
a                                                                                               &= \dfrac{12 \times \cancel {8} \times 4} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
a                                                                                               &= 12 \times 4 \\\\
a                                                                                               &= 48 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Father's age $= 48 \ \text {years}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In September $2015$ Mary was seven years older than Anna and Rebecca was three years older than Mary.

In September $2018$ Rebecca's age is three times Anna's age.  

How old were the three girls in September $2015 \text{?}$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In September $2015$ Mary was seven years older than Anna and Rebecca was three years older than Mary.
<emptyline>
In September $2018$ Rebecca's age is three times Anna's age.  
<emptyline>
How old were the three girls in September $2015 \text{?}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Mary's age in September $2015 = a$

Anna's age $= a - 7$

Rebecca's age $= a + 3$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 3 + 3               &= 3 \times (a - 7 + 3) \\\\
a + 6                   &= 3 \times (a - 4) \\\\
a + 6                   &= 3a - 12 \\\\
3a - 12                 &= a + 6 \\\\
3a - a                  &= 6 + 12 \\\\
2a                      &= 18 \\\\
a                       &= 9 \\\\
\text {Mary's age}      &= 9 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Anna's age}      &= a - 7 \\\\
                        &= 9 - 7 \\\\
                        &= 2 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Rebecca's age}   &= a + 3 \\\\
                        &= 9 + 3 \\\\
                        &= 12 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Mary $=$ $9 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Anna $=$ $2 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Rebecca $=$ $12 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In November $2006$ Becky is two and a half times Anna's age and Mary is twice Anna's age. 

In November $2009$ Anna is half of Becky's age. Mary is six years older than Anna. 

How old are the three girls in November $2009 \text{?}$  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In November $2006$ Becky is two and a half times Anna's age and Mary is twice Anna's age. 
<emptyline>
In November $2009$ Anna is half of Becky's age. Mary is six years older than Anna. 
<emptyline>
How old are the three girls in November $2009 \text{?}$  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Anna's age in $2006$ $= a$

Becky's age $= 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \times a$

Mary's age $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (a + 3)                                        &= 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \times a + 3 \\\\
2a + 6                                                  &= \dfrac {5a} {2} + 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} + 3                                     &= 2a + 6 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} - 2a                                    &= 6 - 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} - \dfrac {2a \times 2} {2}              &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} - \dfrac {4a} {2}                       &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a - 4a} {2}                                    &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac {a} {2}                                          &= 3 \\\\
a                                                       &= 3 \times 2 \\\\
a                                                       &= 6 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the age of the three girls in $2009$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Anna's age}  &= a + 3 \\\\
                    &= 6 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 9 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Mary's age}  &= 2a + 3 \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 6 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 12 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 15 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Becky's age} &= \dfrac {5a} {2} + 3 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 6} {2} + 3 \\\\
                    &= 5 \times 3 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 15 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 18 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Anna $=$ $9 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Mary $=$ $15 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Becky $=$ $18 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
Mary's age $= 2a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times (a + 3)                                        &= 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \times a + 3 \\\\
2a + 6                                                  &= \dfrac {5a} {2} + 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} + 3                                     &= 2a + 6 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} - 2a                                    &= 6 - 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} - \dfrac {2a \times 2} {2}              &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a} {2} - \dfrac {4a} {2}                       &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac {5a - 4a} {2}                                    &= 3 \\\\
\dfrac {a} {2}                                          &= 3 \\\\
a                                                       &= 3 \times 2 \\\\
a                                                       &= 6 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's find the age of the three girls in $2009$.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Anna's age}  &= a + 3 \\\\
                    &= 6 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 9 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Mary's age}  &= 2a + 3 \\\\
                    &= 2 \times 6 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 12 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 15 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Becky's age} &= \dfrac {5a} {2} + 3 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 6} {2} + 3 \\\\
                    &= 5 \times 3 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 15 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 18 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In November $2007$ Becky is six times Anna's age and Mary is four times Anna's age. 

In November $2009$ Anna is a quarter of Becky's age. Mary is six years younger than Becky. 

How old are the three girls in November $2009 \text{?}$  

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In November $2007$ Becky is six times Anna's age and Mary is four times Anna's age. 
<emptyline>
In November $2009$ Anna is a quarter of Becky's age. Mary is six years younger than Becky. 
<emptyline>
How old are the three girls in November $2009 \text{?}$  
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Anna's age in $2007$ $= a$

Becky's age $= 6a$

Mary's age $= 4a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4 \times (a + 2)    &= 6a + 2 \\\\
4a + 8              &= 6a + 2 \\\\
6a + 2              &= 4a + 8 \\\\
6a - 4a             &= 8 - 2 \\\\
2a                  &= 6 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {6} {2} \\\\
a                   &= 3 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the age of the three girls in $2009$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Anna's age}  &= a + 2 \\\\
                    &= 3 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 5 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Becky's age} &= 6a + 2 \\\\
                    &= 6 \times 3 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 18 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 20 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Mary's age}  &= 4a + 2 \\\\
                    &= 4 \times 3 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 12 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 14 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Anna $=$ $5 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Becky $=$ $20 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Mary $=$ $14 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Today Michael is half of Anthony's age and Paul is four 
years older than Michael.

In three years Paul will be the same age as Anthony is today. 

How old are the three boys today? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Today Michael is half of Anthony's age and Paul is four 
years older than Michael.
<emptyline>
In three years Paul will be the same age as Anthony is today. 
<emptyline>
How old are the three boys today? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Anthony's current age $= a$

Michael's age $= \dfrac {a} {2}$

Paul's age $= \dfrac {a} {2} + 4$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a                                                       &= \dfrac {a} {2} + 4 + 3 \\\\
a - \dfrac {a} {2}                                      &= 7 \\\\
\dfrac {a} {2}                                          &= 7 \\\\
a                                                       &= 7 \times 2 \\\\
a                                                       &= 14 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Anthony's age}    &= 14 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Michael's age}   &= \dfrac {a} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {14} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= 7 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Paul's age}      &= \dfrac {a} {2} + 4 \\\\
                        &= 7 + 4 \\\\
                        &= 11 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Anthony $=$ $14 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Michael $=$ $7 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Paul $=$ $11 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Alan's age $= a$

Let Ben's age $= b$

Let Carl's age $= c$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + b       &= 43 \\\\
a + c       &= 55 \\\\
b + c       &= 66
\end{aligned}
$

Let's add all the equations to find the combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl.

$
\begin{aligned}
(a + b) + (b + c) + (a + c) &= 43 + 66 + 55 \\\\
2a + 2b + 2c                &= 164 \\\\
2 \times (a + b + c)        &= 164 \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {164} {2} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 82} {2} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 82} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a + b + c                   &= 82 \ \text{years}
\end{aligned}
$

Combined age of Alan, Ben and Carl $= 82 \ \text{years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$82 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
