<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How old is Alan?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How old is Alan?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + b + c   &= 82 \\\\
b + c       &= 66
\end{aligned}
$

Let's substitute $b$ and $c$ from the second equation to reduce it to just one variable. 

$
\begin{aligned}
a + (b + c)   &= 82 \\\\
a + 66        &= 82 \\\\
a             &= 82 - 66 \\\\
a             &= 16 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Alan's age $= 16 \ \text {years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On $1^\text{st}$ December $2008$ my grandmother was $80$ years old. Her daughter was $40$ years old on $1^\text{st}$ December $1996$. How old was my grandmother when her daughter was born?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On $1^\text{st}$ December $2008$ my grandmother was $80$ years old. Her daughter was $40$ years old on $1^\text{st}$ December $1996$. How old was my grandmother when her daughter was born?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Grandmother's age in 2008}               &= 80 \\\\
\text {Daughter's age in 2008}                  &= 40 + (2008 - 1996) \\\\
                                                &= 40 + 12 \\\\
                                                &= 52 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Grandmother's age at daughter's birth}    &= 80 - 52 \\\\
                                                &= 28 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$28 \ \text{years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Hermione is $11$ years old and her mother is $43$ years old. How old will Hermione be when her mother is $12$ times as old as 
Hermione was $7$ years ago?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Hermione is $11$ years old and her mother is $43$ years old. How old will Hermione be when her mother is $12$ times as old as 
Hermione was $7$ years ago?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Mother's age}    &= 12 \times \text {Hermione's age} \ 7 \ \text {years ago} \\\\
                        &= 12 \times (11 - 7) \\\\
                        &= 12 \times 4 \\\\
                        &= 48 \ \text {years} \\\\
\text {Hermione's age}  &= 11 + (48 - 43) \\\\
                        &= 11 + 5 \\\\
                        &= 16 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>

                        &= 12 \times (11 - 7) \\\\
                        &= 12 \times 4 \\\\
                        &= 48 \ \text {years} \\\\

                        &= 11 + 5 \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Alison, Bethany and Catherine are three sisters. 
They are $6$ years old, $7$ years old and $12$ years old. 

Bethany is older than Alison. 
Catherine's age is a prime number. 

What is each girl's age?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Alison, Bethany and Catherine are three sisters. 
They are $6$ years old, $7$ years old and $12$ years old. 
<emptyline>
Bethany is older than Alison. 
Catherine's age is a prime number. 
<emptyline>
What is each girl's age?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As Catherine's age is a prime number,
Catherine is $ 7 \ \text{years}$ old.

Bethany is older than Alison,
so Bethany is $12 \ \text {years}$ old.

Hence, Alison is $6 \ \text {years}$ old.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Alison $=$ $6 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Bethany $=$ $12 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Catherine $=$ $7 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Eleanor is $11 \ \text{years}$  and $4 \ \text{months}$  old. Her sister Mary is $3 \ \text{years}$  and $10 \ \text{months}$  younger than Eleanor.

How old is Mary? Give your answer in years and months.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Eleanor is $11 \ \text{years}$  and $4 \ \text{months}$  old. Her sister Mary is $3 \ \text{years}$  and $10 \ \text{months}$  younger than Eleanor.
<emptyline>
How old is Mary? Give your answer in years and months.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Mary's age}  &= \text {Eleanor's age} - 3 \ \text {years} \ 10 \ \text {months} \\\\
                    &= 11 \ \text {years} \ 4 \ \text {months} - 3 \ \text {years} \ 10 \ \text {months} \\\\
                    &= 7 \ \text {years} \ 6 \ \text {months}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7 \ \text {years} \ 6 \ \text {months}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Clara has four brothers.

Sam's age added to Ben's age gives $12$.

The total of Frederick's and Sam's and Ben's ages is $28$.

Mylo's age added to Sam's age gives $20$. 

The total of all the brothers' ages is $39$.

Work out the ages of Clara's brothers.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Clara has four brothers.
<emptyline>
Sam's age added to Ben's age gives $12$.
<emptyline>
The total of Frederick's and Sam's and Ben's ages is $28$.
<emptyline>
Mylo's age added to Sam's age gives $20$. 
<emptyline>
The total of all the brothers' ages is $39$.
<emptyline>
Work out the ages of Clara's brothers.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Sam's age $= s$

Let Ben's age $= b$

Let Frederick's age $= f$

Let Mylo's age $= m$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
s + b           &= 12  & (1) \\\\
s + b + f       &= 28  & (2) \\\\
s + m           &= 20  & (3) \\\\
s + b + f + m   &= 39  & (4)
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find Mylo's age. Using equation $4$ and $2$ we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
s + b + f + m   &= 39 \\\\
(s + b + f) + m &= 39 \\\\
28 + m          &= 39 \\\\
m               &= 39 - 28 \\\\
m               &= 11 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Mylo's age $= 11 \ \text {years}$

Let's find Frederick's age. Using equation $4$ and $1$ we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
s + b + f + m    &= 39 \\\\
(s + b) + f + m  &= 39 \\\\
12 + f + 11      &= 39 \\\\
f + 23           &= 39 \\\\
f                &= 39 - 23 \\\\
f                &= 16 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Frederick's age $= 16 \ \text {years}$

Let's find Sam's age. Using equation $3$ we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
s + m   &= 20 \\\\
s + 11  &= 20 \\\\
s       &= 20 - 11 \\\\
s       &= 9 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Sam's age $= 9 \ \text {years}$

Lets's find Ben's age. Using equation $1$ we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
s + b   &= 12 \\\\
9 + b   &= 12 \\\\
b       &= 12 - 9 \\\\
b       &= 3 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Ben's age $= 3 \ \text {years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Sam $=$ $9 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Ben $=$ $3 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Frederick $=$ $16 \ \text {years}$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Mylo $=$ $11 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let Sam's age $= s$
<emptyline>
Let Ben's age $= b$
<emptyline>
Let Frederick's age $= f$
<emptyline>
Let Mylo's age $= m$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
s + b           &= 12  & (1) \\\\
s + b + f       &= 28  & (2) \\\\
s + m           &= 20  & (3) \\\\
s + b + f + m   &= 39  & (4)
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Let's find Mylo's age. Using equation $4$ and $2$ we get,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
s + b + f + m   &= 39 \\\\
(s + b + f) + m &= 39 \\\\
28 + m          &= 39 \\\\
m               &= 39 - 28 \\\\
m               &= 11 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Mylo's age $= 11 \ \text {years}$
<emptyline>
Let's find Frederick's age. Using equation $4$ and $1$ we get,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
s + b + f + m    &= 39 \\\\
(s + b) + f + m  &= 39 \\\\
12 + f + 11      &= 39 \\\\
f + 23           &= 39 \\\\
f                &= 39 - 23 \\\\
f                &= 16 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Frederick's age $= 16 \ \text {years}$
<emptyline>
Let's find Sam's age. Using equation $3$ we get,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
s + m   &= 20 \\\\
s + 11  &= 20 \\\\
s       &= 20 - 11 \\\\
s       &= 9 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Sam's age $= 9 \ \text {years}$
<emptyline>
Lets's find Ben's age. Using equation $1$ we get,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
s + b   &= 12 \\\\
9 + b   &= 12 \\\\
b       &= 12 - 9 \\\\
b       &= 3 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Ben's age $= 3 \ \text {years}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Anna's age is twice as that of David's and half that of 
Preeya's. The total of all their ages is $21$.

How old is Preeya?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Anna's age is twice as that of David's and half that of 
Preeya's. The total of all their ages is $21$.
<emptyline>
How old is Preeya?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Anna's age $= a$

David's age $= \dfrac {a} {2}$

Preeya's age $= 2a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + \dfrac {a} {2} + 2a                                                                 &= 21 \\\\
\dfrac {2 \times a} {2} + \dfrac {a} {2} + \dfrac {2 \times 2a } {2}                    &= 21 \\\\
\dfrac {2a} {2} + \dfrac {a} {2} + \dfrac {4a} {2}                                      &= 21 \\\\
\dfrac {2a + a + 4a} {2}                                                                &= 21 \\\\
\dfrac {7a} {2}                                                                         &= 21 \\\\
a                                                                                       &= \dfrac {21 \times 2} {7} \\\\
a                                                                                       &= \dfrac {7 \times 3 \times 2} {7} \\\\
a                                                                                       &= \dfrac {\cancel {7} \times 3 \times 2} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
a                                                                                       &= 3 \times 2 \\\\
a                                                                                       &= 6 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Preeya's age}    &= 2a \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 6 \\\\
                        &= 12 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Preetesh is taller than Davina who is taller than Alicia.

The difference in height between Pritesh and Davina 
is the same as the difference in height between Davina and Alicia. 

The total of their heights is $300 \ \text{cm}$. 

How tall is Davina?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Preetesh is taller than Davina who is taller than Alicia.
<emptyline>
The difference in height between Pritesh and Davina 
is the same as the difference in height between Davina and Alicia. 
<emptyline>
The total of their heights is $300 \ \text{cm}$. 
<emptyline>
How tall is Davina?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Preetesh's height $= p$

Let Davina's height $= d$

Let Alicia's height $= a$

As per the equation,

$
\begin{aligned}
p - d           &= d - a \\\\
p + a           &= d + d \\\\
p + a           &= 2d       & (1) \\\\
p + a + d       &= 300      & (2)
\end{aligned}
$

Using equation $1$ and equation $2$,

$
\begin{aligned}
(p + a) + d &= 300 \\\\
2d + d      &= 300 \\\\
3d          &= 300 \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {300} {3} \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {3} \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 100} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
d           &= 100 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

Davina's height $= 100 \ \text{cm}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let Preetesh's height $= p$
<emptyline>
Let Davina's height $= d$
<emptyline>
Let Alicia's height $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the equation,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
p - d           &= d - a \\\\
p + a           &= d + d \\\\
p + a           &= 2d       & (1) \\\\
p + a + d       &= 300      & (2)
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Using equation $1$ and equation $2$,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
(p + a) + d &= 300 \\\\
2d + d      &= 300 \\\\
3d          &= 300 \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {300} {3} \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {3} \\\\
d           &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 100} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
d           &= 100 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Davina's height $= 100 \ \text{cm}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$100 \ \text{cm}$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Kitty is $8 \ \text{years}$ and $5 \ \text{months}$ old.

Rover is $5 \ \text{years}$ and $8 \ \text{months}$ younger than Kitty.

How old is Rover?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Kitty is $8 \ \text{years}$ and $5 \ \text{months}$ old.
<emptyline>
Rover is $5 \ \text{years}$ and $8 \ \text{months}$ younger than Kitty.
<emptyline>
How old is Rover?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Rover's age}     &= \text {Kitty's age} - 5 \ \text {years} \ 8 \ \text {months} \\\\
                        &= 8 \ \text {years} \ 5 \ \text {months} - 5 \ \text {years} \ 8 \ \text {months} \\\\
                        &= 2 \ \text {years} \ 9 \ \text {months}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \ \text {years} \ 9 \ \text {months}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Becky is $10 \dfrac{1}{2} \ \text{years}$ old.

Her brother is exactly $1 \ \text{year}$ and $8 \ \text{months}$ younger than Becky.

How old is her brother?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Becky is $10 \dfrac{1}{2} \ \text{years}$ old.
<emptyline>
Her brother is exactly $1 \ \text{year}$ and $8 \ \text{months}$ younger than Becky.
<emptyline>
How old is her brother?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Becky's age}      &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Brother's age}    &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} - 1 \ \text {years} \ 8 \ \text {months} \\\\
                        &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} - 1 \dfrac {8} {12} \ \text {years} \\\\
                        &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} - 1 \dfrac {2} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {21} {2} - \dfrac {5} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {21 \times 3} {2 \times 3} - \dfrac {5 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {63} {6} - \dfrac {10} {6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {63 - 10} {6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {53} {6} \\\\
                        &= 8 \dfrac {5} {6} \ \text {years} \\\\
                        &= 8 \ \text {years} \ \dfrac {5} {6} \times 12 \ \text {months} \\\\
                        &= 8 \ \text {years } 10 \ \text {months}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8 \ \text {years } 10 \ \text {months}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Becky's age}      &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} \\\\
\text{Brother's age}    &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} - 1 \ \text {years} \ 8 \ \text {months} \\\\
                        &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {years} - 1 \dfrac {8} {12} \ \text {years} \\\\
                        &= 10 \dfrac {1} {2} - 1 \dfrac {2} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {21} {2} - \dfrac {5} {3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {21 \times 3} {2 \times 3} - \dfrac {5 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {63} {6} - \dfrac {10} {6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {63 - 10} {6} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {53} {6} \\\\
                        &= 8 \dfrac {5} {6} \ \text {years} \\\\
                        &= 8 \ \text {years} \ \dfrac {5} {6} \times 12 \ \text {months} \\\\
                        &= 8 \ \text {years } 10 \ \text {months}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$8 \ \text {years } 10 \ \text {months}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

George is $2$ years and $9$ months older than his sister Sophie.

George is $12$ years and $5$ months old.

How old is Sophie?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">George is $2$ years and $9$ months older than his sister Sophie.
<emptyline>
George is $12$ years and $5$ months old.
<emptyline>
How old is Sophie?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sophie's age}  &= \text {George's age} - 2 \ \text {years} \ 9 \ \text {months} \\\\
                      &= 12 \ \text {years} \ 5 \ \text {months} - 2 \ \text {years} \ 9 \ \text {months} \\\\
                      &= 9 \ \text {years} \ 8 \ \text {months}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9 \ \text {years} \ 8 \ \text {months}$

</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The composer Johann Sebastian Bach lived for $65$ years and $4$ months.

The composer Georg Philipp Telemann lived for $86$ years and $3$ months.

For how much longer did Telemann live than Bach?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The composer Johann Sebastian Bach lived for $65$ years and $4$ months.
<emptyline>
The composer Georg Philipp Telemann lived for $86$ years and $3$ months.
<emptyline>
For how much longer did Telemann live than Bach?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}      &= \text {Years Telemann lived} - \text {Years Bach lived} \\\\
                        &= 86 \ \text {years} \ 3 \ \text {months} - 65 \ \text {years} \ 4 \ \text {months} \\\\
                        &= 20 \ \text {years} \ 11 \ \text {months}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \ \text {years} \ 11 \ \text {months}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is Joanna's age, on $1 ^\text{st}$ January, in $2016$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is Joanna's age, on $1 ^\text{st}$ January, in $2016$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Joanna's age}    &= 1 \text {st January} \ 2016 - 19 \text {th} \ \text {August} \ 2004 \\\\
					    &= 11 \ \text {years} \ 4 \ \text {months} \ 13 \ \text {days}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In which year, on $1 ^\text{st}$ January, will Wendy's age be three times Joanna's age?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In which year, on $1 ^\text{st}$ January, will Wendy's age be three times Joanna's age?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Joanna's age in that year $= a$

Wendy's age in that year $= 3a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3a      &= a + 26 \\\\
3a - a  &= 26 \\\\
2a      &= 26 \\\\
a       &= \dfrac {26} {2} \\\\
a       &= \dfrac {2 \times 13} {2} \\\\
a       &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 13} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a       &= 13 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Joanna will be $13 \ \text {years}$ in August of year $2004 + 13 = 2017$.

But we need to tell on $1 \text{st}$ January, so we need to increment the year by $1$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Year}    &= 2004 + 13 + 1 \\\\
                &= 2018
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2018$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let Joanna's age in that year $= a$
<emptyline>
Wendy's age in that year $= 3a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>

a       &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 13} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a       &= 13 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Joanna will be $13 \ \text {years}$ in August of year $2004 + 13 = 2017$.
<emptyline>
But we need to tell on $1 \text{st}$ January, so we need to increment the year by $1$.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Year}    &= 2004 + 13 + 1 \\\\
                &= 2018
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The total of the ages of a father, his daughter and his two sons is $96\ \text{years}$. 

The daughter is half the father's age and double the age of her two brothers. 

How old is the father?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The total of the ages of a father, his daughter and his two sons is $96\ \text{years}$. 
<emptyline>
The daughter is half the father's age and double the age of her two brothers. 
<emptyline>
How old is the father?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the father's age $= a$

Daughter's age $= \dfrac {a} {2}$

Brothers' ages $= \dfrac {a} {4}$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + \dfrac {a} {2} + \dfrac {a} {4} + \dfrac {a} {4}                                                     &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {a \times 4} {4} + \dfrac {a \times 2} {2 \times 2} + \dfrac{a} {4} + \dfrac{a} {4}               &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {4a} {4} + \dfrac {2a} {4} + \dfrac {a} {4} + \dfrac {a} {4}                             		 &= 96 \\\\ 
\dfrac {4a + 2a + a + a} {4}                                                                    		 &= 96 \\\\
\dfrac {8a} {4}                                                                                 		 &= 96 \\\\
8a                                                                                              		 &= 96 \times 4 \\\\
a                                                                                               		 &= \dfrac {96 \times 4} {8} \\\\
a                                                                                               		 &= \dfrac {96 \times 4} {8} \\\\
a                                                                                               		 &= \dfrac {8 \times 12 \times 4} {8} \\\\
a                                                                                               		 &= \dfrac {\cancel 8 \times 12 \times 4} {\cancel 8} \\\\
a                                                                                               		 &= 12 \times 4 \\\\
a                                                                                               		 &= 48 \ \text {years}
\end{aligned}
$

Father's age $= 48 \ \text {years}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$48 \ \text {years}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
